📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期诺如病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 诺如病毒 GII.4 的总体感染率为每 1000 人年 205 例感染(后中位数;95% 可信区间:191-210),跨年范围为每 1000 人年 36-486 例感染(后中位数范围)。
  • 英国卫生安全局的报告显示,过去两周,英格兰的 诺如病毒 病例增加了 35% 以上,总病例数比典型季节性水平高出约 5%。苏格兰的病例有所下降,但威尔士的病例有所增加。 [10.64628/ab.tkxj43hyq]。
  • 在 1353 名成人和 93 名儿童中,63% 的成人和 61% 的儿童返回了血液样本。其中,88% 被认为可以进行处理,100% 的可行样本具有足够的体积和质量,可以进行 诺如病毒 抗体的处理和评估。
  • 在猫、狗、几种鸭类和鸡的血清中观察到针对多种抗原的高抗体滴度,而在大鼠、猪、马和野猪的血清中观察到滴度较低、基因型较少的抗体。来自猕猴、海狮、港湾鼠海豚、牛、绵羊、山羊和血清素蝙蝠的血清显示出低或不可检测的抗体滴度。九十五个样本表现出免疫显性反应,定义为一到三个基因型表现出比其余基因型的几何平均值更高的抗体滴度。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Overall infection rates with norovirus GII.4 were 205 infections per 1000 person-years (posterior median; 95% credible intervals: 191–210) with a range across years of 36–486 infections per 1000 person-years (range of posterior medians).
  • Reports from the UK Health Security Agency indicate that norovirus cases in England have increased by more than 35% over the past two weeks, with overall case numbers about 5% above typical seasonal levels.
  • Of the 1353 adults and 93 children, 63% of adults and 61% of children returned a blood specimen.
  • High antibody titers to multiple antigens were observed in sera from cats, dogs, and several duck species, and chickens, whereas lower titers to fewer genotypes were observed in sera from rats, pigs, horses, and wild boar.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期诺如病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 对 2,161 篇有关儿科 诺如病毒 感染的出版物进行了全面的文献计量分析,揭示了研究成果和合作网络的趋势。Bioengineer.org 将这项研究作为科学评论进行了报道。 [news-09585b6a40e6a127]。
  • 一项数学建模研究评估了英格兰儿童急性胃肠炎的诺如病毒 GII.4 血清学样本,揭示了高感染率和免疫印迹效应。该研究结果由Bioengineer.org于2026年11月23日报道。 [news-68e4e03776a7832a]。
  • 一项广泛的分子 监测 研究在中国 200 个灌溉用水点样本中的 17 个中检测到了 监测 污染,与春季相比,冬季的检测率出现了明显的峰值。该研究于2026年11月21日由Bioengineer.org报道,并根据分子监测的结果得到证实。
  • 据 WBTV 报道,北卡罗来纳州一所小学的 100 多名学生和教职员工出现胃肠道症状。 [news-8cf5a51cd3b49679]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Norovirus news developments.
  • A comprehensive bibliometric analysis of 2,161 publications on pediatric norovirus infections was conducted, revealing trends in research output and collaboration networks.
  • A mathematical modeling study evaluated pediatric norovirus GII.4 serology samples in England, revealing high infection rates and immune imprinting effects.
  • An extensive molecular surveillance study detected norovirus contamination in seventeen of two hundred sampled irrigation water sites across China, showing a marked winter peak in detection rates compared to spring.
  • A norovirus outbreak caused gastrointestinal symptoms in over 100 students and staff at a North Carolina elementary school, as reported by WBTV. [news-8cf5a51cd3b49679].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLoS Pathogens  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-23  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.ppat.1014059  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 11, e1014059
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

根据英文 血清学 数据估算 诺如病毒 GII.4 变体的年度暴露量和抗体动力学,2007-2012 年

Estimation of annual exposures and antibody kinetics against norovirus GII.4 variants from English serology data, 2007–2012
作者： Kathleen M. O’Reilly, James A. Hay, Lisa C. Lindesmith, David James Allen, Stéphane Hué, Kari M. Debbink, Adam J. Kucharski, Ralph S. Baric, Judith Breuer, W. John Edmunds
摘要中文翻译诺如病毒 在人类中具有高度传染性,导致腹泻和呕吐,在幼儿中尤其常见。冬季发病率每年都会发生变化,之前的研究表明,显性发病率变异的变化之后是高发病率,但有一个明确的机制来解释这一观察结果可以支持更好地预测流行病。在这里,我们分析了独特的 诺如病毒 GII.4 血清学 封锁数据,这些数据来自 2007 年至 2012 年期间通过机会性抽样收集的 656 名英格兰儿童,使用多变体抗体动力学数学模型来推断年度发病率和特定年龄感染率等指标。分析显示,诺如病毒 GII.4 的总体感染率为每 1000 人年 205 例感染(后中位数;95% 可信区间:191-210),跨年范围为每 1000 人年 36-486 例感染(后中位数范围)。1 岁以下儿童的感染率最低,每 1000 人年有 161 例感染(95% CrI:121-210),5 岁及以上儿童感染率最高,每 1000 人年有 250 例感染(95% CrI:213-289)。2002 年的年度攻击率最高,与主要变种向法明顿山的转变同时发生,并且经常观察到新变种的出现,但并非总是如此。参数估计表明免疫印记假说有适度的证据:对生命早期遇到的变异有更强的抗体反应。这里估计的感染率高于 发病率 在仅基于疾病的类似环境中报告的感染率,并且与大量无症状感染一致。多变体抗体数据和数学模型的结合使用提供了关于 诺如病毒 变体自然史的重要见解,可以为流行病规划提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 2007-2012 年英文 血清学 数据中针对 诺如病毒 GII.4 变体的年度暴露和抗体动力学,以支持更好地预测 诺如病毒 流行病。
设计与对象
该研究使用多变体抗体动力学数学模型分析了 2007 年至 2012 年间收集的 656 名英国儿童的 血清学 数据。
核心方法
使用多变体抗体动力学的数学模型从独特的 诺如病毒 GII.4 血清学 封锁数据推断年度发病率和特定年龄感染率等指标。
主要结果
诺如病毒 GII.4 的总体感染率为每 1000 人年 205 例感染(后中位数;95% 可信区间:191-210),跨年范围为每 1000 人年 36-486 例感染(后中位数范围)。
解释与创新
该研究提供了关于 诺如病毒 变种自然史的重要见解,表明年龄较大的儿童的感染率较高,并且 2002 年的年发病率最高,与向法明顿山变种的过渡一致。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为流行病规划提供信息,并强调了解免疫印记假说在预测 诺如病毒 暴发 中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究对免疫印记假说有适度的证据,但承认参数估计是基于独特的 血清学 数据,并且研究结果的普遍性可能有限。

Estimation of annual exposures and antibody kinetics against norovirus GII.4 variants from English serology data, 2007–2012

Authors: Kathleen M. O’Reilly, James A. Hay, Lisa C. Lindesmith, David James Allen, Stéphane Hué, Kari M. Debbink, Adam J. Kucharski, Ralph S. Baric, Judith Breuer, W. John Edmunds
Original abstract (English)
Norovirus in humans is highly contagious, causing diarrhoea and vomiting, and is especially common in young children. Winter incidence varies annually, and previous research indicates that the change of dominant norovirus variant is followed by high incidence, but having a clear mechanism to explain this observation could support better prediction of epidemics. Here we analyse unique norovirus GII.4 serology blockade data from 656 children in England collected via opportunistic sampling between 2007–2012 using a mathematical model of multi-variant antibody kinetics to infer metrics such as annual attack rates and age-specific infection rates. Analysis reveals that overall infection rates with norovirus GII.4 were 205 infections per 1000 person-years (posterior median; 95% credible intervals: 191–210) with a range across years of 36–486 infections per 1000 person-years (range of posterior medians). Infection rates were lowest in children aged under 1 year at 161 infections per 1000 person-years (95% CrI: 121–210) and highest in children aged 5 years and older, at 250 infections per 1000 person-years (95% CrI: 213–289). The annual attack rate was highest in 2002, coincident with transition of the dominant variant to Farmington Hills, and frequently observed with emergence of new variants, but not always. Parameter estimates indicate moderate evidence for the immune imprinting hypothesis: a stronger antibody response to variants encountered earliest in life. Infection rates estimated here are higher than incidence reported within similar settings based on disease only and is consistent with considerable asymptomatic infection. The combined use of multi-variant antibody data and a mathematical model provide key insights on the natural history of norovirus variants which can inform epidemic planning.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the annual exposures and antibody kinetics against norovirus GII.4 variants from English serology data, 2007–2012, to support better prediction of norovirus epidemics.
Design and population
The study analysed serology data from 656 children in England collected between 2007 and 2012 using a mathematical model of multi-variant antibody kinetics.
Core methods
A mathematical model of multi-variant antibody kinetics was used to infer metrics such as annual attack rates and age-specific infection rates from unique norovirus GII.4 serology blockade data.
Main results
Overall infection rates with norovirus GII.4 were 205 infections per 1000 person-years (posterior median; 95% credible intervals: 191–210) with a range across years of 36–486 infections per 1000 person-years (range of posterior medians).
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides key insights on the natural history of norovirus variants, indicating that infection rates are higher in older children and that the annual attack rate is highest in 2002, coincident with the transition to the Farmington Hills variant.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform epidemic planning and highlight the importance of understanding the immune imprinting hypothesis in predicting norovirus outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has moderate evidence for the immune imprinting hypothesis but acknowledges that parameter estimates are based on unique serology data and that the generalisability of the findings may be limited.

动物体内对人 诺如病毒 的抗体反应分析

Profiling of antibody responses to human norovirus in animals
作者： Stefan T. van der Krieken, Nele Villabruna, Claudia M. E. Schapendonk, Felicity D. Chandler, Ray W. Izquierdo-Lara, Reina S. Sikkema, Rik L. de Swart, Lineke Begeman, Marco W. G. van de Bildt, Miriam Maas
摘要中文翻译摘要 诺如病毒 是全球 急性胃肠炎 的主要原因,有 30 多种基因型在人类中传播,其中一些广泛传播,另一些则零星检测到。人类 诺如病毒 已在多种动物物种中检测到,这提高了动物可能充当新兴人类 诺如病毒 品系的 储存宿主 或中间体 宿主 的可能性。为了评估 血清学 是否可以揭示人类 诺如病毒 基因型在动物中的循环,我们使用先前建立的包含来自 30 种 GI、GII、GIV 和 GVI 基因型的 P 粒子抗原的多重蛋白质微阵列分析了来自 17 个动物物种的 465 份血清中的抗体反应。在猫、狗、几种鸭类和鸡的血清中观察到针对多种抗原的高抗体滴度,而在大鼠、猪、马和野猪的血清中观察到滴度较低、基因型较少的抗体。来自猕猴、海狮、港湾鼠海豚、牛、绵羊、山羊和血清素蝙蝠的血清显示出低或不可检测的抗体滴度。尽管我们在猫和狗中检测到了针对常见人类基因型的抗体,但其他动物物种中的抗体主要针对零星报告的基因型。九十五个样本表现出免疫显性反应,定义为一到三个基因型表现出比其余基因型的几何平均值更高的抗体滴度,因此暗示基因型特异性识别。对选定血清进行的结合阻断测定证实了在阵列上观察到的一些抗体反应。尽管我们没有发现人类 诺如病毒 在动物中循环的明确证据,但在猫、狗和鸟类中观察到的强烈且广泛反应的抗体谱值得进一步研究。重要性 人类 诺如病毒 已在多种动物物种中检测到,引起人们的担忧,即动物可能充当新兴人类 诺如病毒 菌株的 储存宿主 或中间体 宿主。然而,目前尚不清楚动物的血清学反应是否反映了人类 诺如病毒 的真实循环或交叉反应抗体识别。使用涵盖 30 个 诺如病毒 基因型的多重蛋白质微阵列,我们分析了 17 个动物物种血清中的抗体反应。我们在猫、狗和几种鸟类中检测到广泛的抗体反应,但几乎没有发现一致的 诺如病毒 基因型特异性识别的证据。结合阻断测定证实了一些检测到的抗体反应。总之,我们的研究结果表明,动物中的大多数抗体反应可能反映了交叉反应或非特异性结合,而不是人类 诺如病毒 的广泛循环。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
诺如病毒 是全球 急性胃肠炎 的主要原因,有 30 多种基因型在人类中传播,其中一些广泛传播,另一些则零星检测到。然而,目前尚不清楚动物的血清学反应是否反映了人类 诺如病毒 的真实循环或交叉反应抗体识别。
设计与对象
为了评估 血清学 是否可以揭示人类 诺如病毒 基因型在动物中的循环,我们使用先前建立的包含来自 30 种 GI、GII、GIV 和 GVI 基因型的 P 粒子抗原的多重蛋白质微阵列分析了来自 17 个动物物种的 465 份血清中的抗体反应。
核心方法
使用涵盖 30 个 诺如病毒 基因型的多重蛋白质微阵列,我们分析了 17 个动物物种血清中的抗体反应。对选定血清进行的结合阻断测定证实了一些检测到的抗体反应。
主要结果
在猫、狗、几种鸭类和鸡的血清中观察到针对多种抗原的高抗体滴度,而在大鼠、猪、马和野猪的血清中观察到滴度较低、基因型较少的抗体。来自猕猴、海狮、港湾鼠海豚、牛、绵羊、山羊和血清素蝙蝠的血清显示出低或不可检测的抗体滴度。九十五个样本表现出免疫显性反应,定义为一到三个基因型表现出比其余基因型的几何平均值更高的抗体滴度。
解释与创新
尽管我们在猫和狗中检测到了针对常见人类基因型的抗体,但其他动物物种中的抗体主要针对零星报告的基因型。总之,我们的研究结果表明,动物中的大多数抗体反应可能反映了交叉反应或非特异性结合,而不是人类 诺如病毒 的广泛循环。
科研与公卫意义
在猫、狗和鸟类中观察到的强而广泛的反应性抗体谱值得进一步研究,以确定这些反应是否表明人类 诺如病毒 的真实循环或归因于交叉反应性。这些发现可能为人类 诺如病毒 传播 和动物的 监测 策略和风险评估提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告人类 诺如病毒 在动物中循环的明确证据,并且研究设计不包括直接病毒检测方法。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于可能反映交叉反应而不是真实循环的血清学反应。

Profiling of antibody responses to human norovirus in animals

Authors: Stefan T. van der Krieken, Nele Villabruna, Claudia M. E. Schapendonk, Felicity D. Chandler, Ray W. Izquierdo-Lara, Reina S. Sikkema, Rik L. de Swart, Lineke Begeman, Marco W. G. van de Bildt, Miriam Maas
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Noroviruses are a major cause of acute gastroenteritis globally, with over 30 genotypes circulating in humans, some widespread, and others sporadically detected. Human noroviruses have been detected in a variety of animal species, raising the possibility that animals may serve as reservoirs or intermediate hosts for emerging human norovirus strains. To assess whether serology can reveal the circulation of human norovirus genotypes in animals, we analyzed antibody responses in 465 sera from 17 animal species using a previously established multiplex protein microarray containing P-particle antigens from 30 GI, GII, GIV, and GVI genotypes. High antibody titers to multiple antigens were observed in sera from cats, dogs, and several duck species, and chickens, whereas lower titers to fewer genotypes were observed in sera from rats, pigs, horses, and wild boar. Sera from macaques, sea lions, harbor porpoises, cattle, sheep, goats, and serotine bats showed low or undetectable antibody titers. Although we detected antibodies to common human genotypes in cats and dogs, antibodies in other animal species were primarily directed against sporadically reported genotypes. Ninety-five samples exhibited immunodominant responses, defined as one to three genotypes exhibiting higher antibody titers than the geometric mean of the remaining genotypes, and therefore suggestive of genotype-specific recognition. Binding-blocking assays performed on selected sera confirmed some antibody responses observed on the array. Although we find no clear evidence of human norovirus circulation in animals, the strong and broadly reactive antibody profiles observed in cats, dogs, and birds warrant further investigation. IMPORTANCE Human noroviruses have been detected in multiple animal species, raising concerns that animals could act as reservoirs or intermediate hosts for emerging human norovirus strains. However, it remains unclear whether serological responses in animals reflect true circulation of human noroviruses or instead cross-reactive antibody recognition. Using a multiplex protein microarray spanning 30 norovirus genotypes, we profiled antibody responses in sera from 17 animal species. We detected broad antibody responses in cats, dogs, and several bird species, but found little evidence of consistent norovirus genotype-specific recognition. Binding-blocking assays confirmed some of the detected antibody responses. Together, our findings suggest that most antibody responses in animals likely reflect cross-reactivity or non-specific binding rather than widespread circulation of human noroviruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Noroviruses are a major cause of acute gastroenteritis globally, with over 30 genotypes circulating in humans, some widespread, and others sporadically detected. However, it remains unclear whether serological responses in animals reflect true circulation of human noroviruses or instead cross-reactive antibody recognition.
Design and population
To assess whether serology can reveal the circulation of human norovirus genotypes in animals, we analyzed antibody responses in 465 sera from 17 animal species using a previously established multiplex protein microarray containing P-particle antigens from 30 GI, GII, GIV, and GVI genotypes.
Core methods
Using a multiplex protein microarray spanning 30 norovirus genotypes, we profiled antibody responses in sera from 17 animal species. Binding-blocking assays performed on selected sera confirmed some of the detected antibody responses.
Main results
High antibody titers to multiple antigens were observed in sera from cats, dogs, and several duck species, and chickens, whereas lower titers to fewer genotypes were observed in sera from rats, pigs, horses, and wild boar. Sera from macaques, sea lions, harbor porpoises, cattle, sheep, goats, and serotine bats showed low or undetectable antibody titers. Ninety-five samples exhibited immunodominant responses, defined as one to three genotypes exhibiting higher antibody titers than the geometric mean of the remaining genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
Although we detected antibodies to common human genotypes in cats and dogs, antibodies in other animal species were primarily directed against sporadically reported genotypes. Together, our findings suggest that most antibody responses in animals likely reflect cross-reactivity or non-specific binding rather than widespread circulation of human noroviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The strong and broadly reactive antibody profiles observed in cats, dogs, and birds warrant further investigation to determine whether these responses indicate true circulation of human noroviruses or are due to cross-reactivity. These findings may inform surveillance strategies and risk assessments for human norovirus transmission from animals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report clear evidence of human norovirus circulation in animals, and the study design does not include direct viral detection methods. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on serological responses that may reflect cross-reactivity rather than true circulation.

自采血样用于 诺如病毒 和 急性胃肠炎 研究的可行性和可用性

Feasibility and Usability of Self-Collected Blood Samples for Studies of Norovirus and Acute Gastroenteritis
作者： Holly C. Groom, Mark A. Schmidt, Jennifer L. Kuntz, Sarah Vertrees, Judy L. Donald, Matthew T. Slaughter, John F. Dickerson, Maureen O’Keeffe-Rosetti, Katherine B. Carlson, Christine Kim
摘要中文翻译背景:Tasso 自采血试剂盒已针对静脉血来源的血清样本进行了验证,但尚未用于 急性胃肠炎 (AGE) 的研究。作者的目标是描述 Tasso+ 在参与医疗 AGE 研究的成人和儿童中自行采集血液样本的性能,并描述样本处理和抗体测试的成功情况。方法:作者在 Kaiser Permanente Northwest 的一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究中使用 Tasso+ 设备自行采集血液样本。评估血清样本中是否存在针对 诺如病毒 衣壳蛋白的抗体。结果:在总共 1353 名成人和 93 名儿童中,63% 的成人和 61% 的儿童返回了血液样本。其中,88% 被认为可以进行处理,100% 的可行样本具有足够的体积和质量,可以进行 诺如病毒 抗体的处理和评估。结论:结果表明,Tasso+ 是一种用于 AGE 研究的可行血液采集工具,有助于作者了解自然感染后的 免疫应答,并可能有助于确定预防严重 诺如病毒 疾病的相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估自我采集血液样本用于 诺如病毒 和 急性胃肠炎 研究的可行性,因为 Tasso 自我采集试剂盒已针对静脉血血清样本进行了验证,但并非专门用于 急性胃肠炎 (AGE) 研究。
设计与对象
作者在 Kaiser Permanente Northwest 进行了一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,招募了 1353 名成人和 93 名接受 急性胃肠炎 治疗的儿童。
核心方法
在前瞻性 队列研究 研究中,Tasso+ 装置用于自行采集血液样本,并评估血清样本中是否存在针对 诺如病毒 衣壳蛋白的抗体。
主要结果
在 1353 名成人和 93 名儿童中,63% 的成人和 61% 的儿童返回了血液样本。其中,88% 被认为可以进行处理,100% 的可行样本具有足够的体积和质量,可以进行 诺如病毒 抗体的处理和评估。
解释与创新
结果表明,Tasso+ 装置是 急性胃肠炎 研究中采集血液样本的可行工具,为了解自然感染后的 免疫应答 并确定预防严重 诺如病毒 疾病的相关性提供了潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,使用 Tasso+ 设备自行采集的血液样本可以通过简化样本采集和处理来改善 诺如病毒 研究的后勤工作,特别是在 急性胃肠炎 研究中。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖自我报告的标本返回以及缺乏有关潜在偏差或混杂因素的详细信息而受到限制,但考虑到所收集标本的高活力和质量,证据是强有力的。

Feasibility and Usability of Self-Collected Blood Samples for Studies of Norovirus and Acute Gastroenteritis

Authors: Holly C. Groom, Mark A. Schmidt, Jennifer L. Kuntz, Sarah Vertrees, Judy L. Donald, Matthew T. Slaughter, John F. Dickerson, Maureen O’Keeffe-Rosetti, Katherine B. Carlson, Christine Kim
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Tasso self-collection blood kits have been validated against venous blood-derived serum samples, but not for studies of acute gastroenteritis (AGE). The authors' objectives were to describe the performance of the Tasso+ for self-collection of blood samples among adults and children enrolled in a study of medically attended AGE and to describe specimen processing and antibody testing success. METHODS: The authors used the Tasso+ device for self-collection of blood samples within a prospective cohort study at Kaiser Permanente Northwest. Serum samples were assessed for the presence of antibodies against the norovirus capsid protein. RESULTS: Of a total of 1353 adults and 93 children, 63% of adults and 61% of children returned a blood specimen. Of these, 88% were deemed viable for processing, and 100% of viable specimens were of sufficient volume and quality to be processed and assessed for norovirus antibodies. CONCLUSIONS: The results suggested that Tasso+ is a feasible blood collection tool for studies of AGE and can contribute to the authors' understanding of immune response after natural infection and potentially help identify correlates of protection against severe norovirus disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the feasibility of self-collected blood samples for studies of norovirus and acute gastroenteritis, as Tasso self-collection kits had been validated against venous blood-derived serum samples but not specifically for acute gastroenteritis (AGE) research.
Design and population
The authors conducted a prospective cohort study at Kaiser Permanente Northwest, enrolling 1353 adults and 93 children who were medically attended for acute gastroenteritis.
Core methods
The Tasso+ device was used for self-collection of blood samples within the prospective cohort study, and serum samples were assessed for the presence of antibodies against the norovirus capsid protein.
Main results
Of the 1353 adults and 93 children, 63% of adults and 61% of children returned a blood specimen. Of these, 88% were deemed viable for processing, and 100% of viable specimens were of sufficient volume and quality to be processed and assessed for norovirus antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The results indicated that the Tasso+ device is a feasible tool for collecting blood samples in studies of acute gastroenteritis, offering potential for understanding immune responses after natural infection and identifying correlates of protection against severe norovirus disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that self-collected blood samples using the Tasso+ device can improve the logistics of norovirus research, particularly in studies of acute gastroenteritis, by enabling easier specimen collection and processing.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported specimen return and the lack of detailed information on potential biases or confounding factors, but the evidence is strong given the high viability and quality of the collected specimens.

中国深圳疫情期间其他感染性腹泻和诺如病毒的季节性变化和趋势

Changing seasonality and trends of other infectious diarrhea and norovirus across the pandemic era in Shenzhen China
作者： Yanpeng Cheng, Huawei Xiong, Zhigao Chen, Jing Ren, 刘婷婷, Jia Zhang, Yang Deng, Yingying Su, Ying wen, Yan Lu
摘要中文翻译其他感染性腹泻 (OID) 是一种异质性 监测 综合征,其时间模式可能会随着 传播、病原体组成、就医和报告的变化而​​变化。我们分析了 2013 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 7 月 31 日期间来自中国深圳的 578,222 份 OID 报告,以及每月 诺如病毒 GI 阳性、GII 阳性和混合 GI + GII 阳性系列。所有四个系列的日历月分布在 COVID-19 之前、COVID-19 期间和 COVID-19 后时期均有所不同。根据温度、相对湿度和雨天频率进行调整的自相关断点分析更有利于 2020 年 2 月,而独立误差分析则有利于 2022 年 1 月。在主要的气候调整 ARMA(2,0) 模型中,OID 报告在 2020 年 2 月下降了 48.1%,然后每月增加 2.15%。斜率变化为每月 1.13%,置信区间为零。2022 年 1 月的敏感性模型估计,突破后的正趋势为每月 3.28%,但其斜率变化也包括零。三段模型发现,与 2020-2021 年相比,2022 年 1 月没有额外的加速。现有的气象协变量无法解释后期的积极趋势。这些发现支持整个 COVID-19 时代 监测 动态的变化,但不是 2022 年独特或因果的加速。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解中国深圳大流行期间其他感染性腹泻 (OID) 和 诺如病毒 的季节性变化和趋势,解决在不断变化的 传播 模式和报告实践中评估 监测 动态的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2013年1月1日至2025年7月31日期间来自中国深圳的578,222份OID报告,以及每月的诺如病毒 GI阳性、GII阳性和混合GI+GII阳性系列。
核心方法
研究人员使用自相关感知断点分析和独立误差分析来分析不同时期的日历月分布。他们还采用 ARMA(2,0) 模型和三段模型来评估趋势和斜率。
主要结果
主要气候调整 ARMA(2,0) 模型显示,2020 年 2 月 OID 报告减少了 48.1%,随后每月增加 2.15%。斜率变化为每月 1.13%,置信区间为零。2022 年 1 月的敏感性模型估计,突破后的正趋势为每月 3.28%,但其斜率变化也包括零。
解释与创新
研究结果支持整个 COVID-19 时代 监测 动态的变化,但不是 2022 年独特或因果的加速。该研究强调了在理解 OID 趋势时考虑多种因素的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以通过提供对 OID 和 诺如病毒 变化模式的见解来为 监测 和公共卫生策略提供信息,这对于有效的疾病管理和预防至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括对报告数据的依赖,这可能会受到寻求护理和报告实践的影响。由于可能存在混杂因素并且需要进一步验证,证据强度中等。

Changing seasonality and trends of other infectious diarrhea and norovirus across the pandemic era in Shenzhen China

Authors: Yanpeng Cheng, Huawei Xiong, Zhigao Chen, Jing Ren, 刘婷婷, Jia Zhang, Yang Deng, Yingying Su, Ying wen, Yan Lu
Original abstract (English)
Other infectious diarrhea (OID) is a heterogeneous surveillance syndrome whose temporal patterns may change with transmission, pathogen composition, care seeking and reporting. We analyzed 578,222 OID reports from Shenzhen, China, from 1 January 2013 to 31 July 2025, together with monthly norovirus GI-positive, GII-positive and mixed GI + GII-positive series. Calendar-month distributions differed across pre-COVID-19, during COVID-19 and post-COVID-19 periods for all four series. Autocorrelation-aware breakpoint profiling with adjustment for temperature, relative humidity and wet-day frequency favored February 2020, whereas independent-error profiling favored January 2022. In the primary climate-adjusted ARMA(2,0) model, OID reports fell 48.1% at February 2020, then increased by 2.15% per month. The slope change was 1.13% per month, with a confidence interval spanning zero. A January 2022 sensitivity model estimated a positive post-break trend of 3.28% per month, but its slope change also included zero. A three-segment model found no additional acceleration in January 2022 relative to 2020–2021. Available meteorological covariates did not explain the positive later trend. These findings support changing surveillance dynamics across the COVID-19 era, but not a unique or causal 2022 acceleration.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the changing seasonality and trends of other infectious diarrhea (OID) and norovirus across the pandemic era in Shenzhen, China, addressing the need to assess surveillance dynamics amidst changing transmission patterns and reporting practices.
Design and population
The study analyzed 578,222 OID reports from Shenzhen, China, from 1 January 2013 to 31 July 2025, alongside monthly norovirus GI-positive, GII-positive, and mixed GI + GII-positive series.
Core methods
The researchers used autocorrelation-aware breakpoint profiling and independent-error profiling to analyze calendar-month distributions across different periods. They also employed an ARMA(2,0) model and a three-segment model to assess trends and slopes.
Main results
The primary climate-adjusted ARMA(2,0) model showed a 48.1% decrease in OID reports at February 2020, followed by a 2.15% monthly increase. The slope change was 1.13% per month, with a confidence interval spanning zero. A January 2022 sensitivity model estimated a positive post-break trend of 3.28% per month, but its slope change also included zero.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support changing surveillance dynamics across the COVID-19 era, but not a unique or causal 2022 acceleration. The study highlights the importance of considering multiple factors in understanding OID trends.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study’s findings can inform surveillance and public health strategies by providing insights into the changing patterns of OID and norovirus, which are crucial for effective disease management and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s design limitations include the reliance on reported data, which may be influenced by care seeking and reporting practices. The evidence strength is moderate due to the potential for confounding factors and the need for further validation.

打破对称性的抗体结合揭示了大流行人类诺如病毒衣壳的广泛可塑性

Symmetry-breaking antibody binding reveals extensive capsid malleability in pandemic human norovirus
作者： Carmen V. Apostol, Ramakrishnan Anish, Son Pham, Khalil Ettayebi, Janam J. Dave, Frederick H. Neill, Marina Bok, Banumathi Sankaran, Sue E. Crawford, Robert L. Atmar
摘要中文翻译病毒衣壳是亚稳态组装体,在稳定性和灵活性之间具有微妙的平衡,对于进入、感染性和免疫逃逸是必需的。然而,人类病毒在多大程度上利用衣壳可塑性以及如何塑造抗原景观仍然知之甚少。人类 诺如病毒 (HuNoV) 是全球急性病毒性胃肠炎的主要原因。GII.4 HuNoV 不断进化,推动了大多数全球流行病,自 2012 年以来,GII.4 悉尼一直是主要变种。主要衣壳蛋白 VP1 是感染性和免疫识别的核心,基于 VP1 的病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 被认为是有前途的 疫苗 候选者。然而,二十面体 HuNoV 衣壳如何在原子水平上响应抗体压力仍不清楚。在这里,我们展示了由冷冻电镜测定的 GII.4 悉尼衣壳的第一个原子结构,揭示了可能有助于其持续主导地位的结构特征。通过集中细化,我们发现了响应中和和非中和抗体的对称性破坏结合的显着衣壳可塑性。VP1 采用 11 种不同的构象模式来适应不对称抗体结合,抗体破坏的 VLP 重新组装成具有 5 种额外 VP1 状态的更小的颗粒。这种之前未被认识到的构象适应性程度为设计针对 HuNoV 感染的下一代基于 VLP 或 mRNA 的 疫苗 提供了关键见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解人类 诺如病毒 (HuNoV) 利用衣壳可塑性的程度以及这如何影响抗原景观,尽管 HuNoV 对全球健康产生了重大影响,但鉴于对这方面的了解有限。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用冷冻电镜测定 GII.4 Sydney 衣壳的原子结构,并分析响应抗体结合的构象变化。
核心方法
研究人员使用冷冻电镜确定了 GII.4 Sydney 衣壳的原子结构,并采用集中细化来揭示衣壳可塑性,以响应中和和非中和抗体的对称破坏结合。
主要结果
研究表明,主要的衣壳蛋白 VP1 采用 11 种不同的构象模式来适应不对称抗体结合,并且抗体破坏的 VLP 重新组装成具有 5 种额外 VP1 状态的更小的颗粒。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对可能有助于 GII.4 Sydney 持续主导地位的结构特征的关键见解,并表明这种先前未被认识的构象适应性程度对于设计针对 HuNoV 感染的下一代 疫苗 至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对 疫苗 设计具有影响,因为它们强调了在开发针对 HuNoV 感染的下一代 疫苗 时考虑衣壳可塑性的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对单一变异的关注和体外方法的使用的限制,这可能无法完全捕捉体内感染的复杂性。由于依赖结构生物学和体外实验,证据强度中等。

Symmetry-breaking antibody binding reveals extensive capsid malleability in pandemic human norovirus

Authors: Carmen V. Apostol, Ramakrishnan Anish, Son Pham, Khalil Ettayebi, Janam J. Dave, Frederick H. Neill, Marina Bok, Banumathi Sankaran, Sue E. Crawford, Robert L. Atmar
Original abstract (English)
Virus capsids are metastable assemblies with an exquisite balance between stability and flexibility, obligatory for entry, infectivity, and immune escape. Yet the extent to which human viruses exploit capsid plasticity and how that consequently shapes antigenic landscapes remains poorly understood. Human noroviruses (HuNoVs) are the leading cause of acute viral gastroenteritis worldwide. GII.4 HuNoVs have been evolving epochally, driving most global pandemics, and since 2012, GII.4 Sydney has been the dominant variant. The major capsid protein, VP1, is central to infectivity and immune recognition, and VP1-based virus-like particles (VLPs) are considered promising vaccine candidates. Yet, how the icosahedral HuNoV capsid responds to antibody pressure at the atomic level remains unclear. Here, we present the first atomic structure of GII.4 Sydney capsid determined by cryo-EM, revealing the structural features that likely contribute to its sustained dominance. Using focused refinement, we uncover the remarkable capsid plasticity in response to symmetry-breaking binding of neutralizing and non-neutralizing antibodies. VP1 adopts 11 distinct conformational modalities to accommodate asymmetric antibody binding, and antibody-disrupted VLPs reassemble into smaller particles with five additional VP1 states. This previously unrecognized extent of conformational adaptability provides key insights for designing next-generation VLP- or mRNA-based vaccines targeting HuNoV infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the extent to which human noroviruses (HuNoVs) exploit capsid plasticity and how this affects antigenic landscapes, given the limited understanding of this aspect despite the significant impact of HuNoVs on global health.
Design and population
The study involved the determination of the atomic structure of GII.4 Sydney capsid using cryo-EM and the analysis of conformational changes in response to antibody binding.
Core methods
The researchers used cryo-EM to determine the atomic structure of the GII.4 Sydney capsid and employed focused refinement to uncover the capsid plasticity in response to symmetry-breaking binding of neutralizing and non-neutralizing antibodies.
Main results
The study revealed that the major capsid protein, VP1, adopts 11 distinct conformational modalities to accommodate asymmetric antibody binding, and antibody-disrupted VLPs reassemble into smaller particles with five additional VP1 states.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide key insights into the structural features that likely contribute to the sustained dominance of GII.4 Sydney, and suggest that this previously unrecognized extent of conformational adaptability is crucial for designing next-generation vaccines targeting HuNoV infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have implications for vaccine design, as they highlight the importance of considering capsid plasticity in the development of next-generation vaccines against HuNoV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the focus on a single variant and the use of in vitro methods, which may not fully capture the complexity of in vivo infections. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on structural biology and in vitro experiments.

2022-2024 年美国成人全因和 诺如病毒 急性胃肠炎 住院期间的临床并发症

Clinical complications during hospitalizations of all-cause and norovirus acute gastroenteritis among adults in the United States, 2022–2024
作者： Christine Kim, John Shen, Brandon J. Patterson, Julia M. Baker, Caroline Bridges, Allison E. Olmsted, Rekha R. Rapaka, Ben Lopman, Daniel C. Payne, Evan J. Anderson
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 尽管 诺如病毒 是全球 急性胃肠炎 (AGE) 的主要原因,但描述住院全因和 诺如病毒 相关 AGE 的临床谱的当代数据有限。我们对全因和 诺如病毒 AGE 成人急性住院期间的临床并发症进行了特征分析。方法 我们使用 Optum 的去识别化 Clinformatics® Data Mart (Optum® CDM) 管理索赔数据库进行了一项描述性研究。使用 ICD-10-CM 诊断 代码识别 2022 年 7 月至 2024 年 6 月期间因全因 AGE 或 诺如病毒 特定事件住院的 18 岁以上成年人。按年龄组(18-59 岁和 ≥ 60 岁)和是否存在基础医疗状况对首发 3 天内发生的急性住院期间的临床并发症进行评估。使用经过验证的基于索赔的虚弱指数评估 60 岁以上成年人的基线虚弱程度。结果 该研究包括 192,430 例住院的全因 AGE 发作和 2,589 例住院的 诺如病毒 编码发作。患者主要是患有多种基础疾病的老年人(中位年龄 74 岁)。代谢紊乱、急性肾损伤、脱水和脓毒症是全因 AGE(分别为 57.2%、38.3%、29.2% 和 26.5%)或 诺如病毒 编码发作(分别为 62.0%、42.6%、41.9% 和 28.1%)患者中最常见的并发症。在 诺如病毒 住院的年轻人中也观察到了较高的并发症发生率。在 60 岁以上的成年人中,有基础疾病的患者基线虚弱程度明显高于没有基础疾病的患者。结论 患有全因和 诺如病毒 编码 AGE 的住院成人通常会出现肾脏、代谢和全身并发症。老年人表现出严重的基线虚弱,而因 诺如病毒 疾病住院的年轻人也出现严重并发症。这些发现改善了严重 AGE(特别是 诺如病毒)的临床特征,并确定了不良结果风险最大的人群。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
诺如病毒 是全球 急性胃肠炎 (AGE) 的主要原因,但有关全因住院期间临床并发症和 诺如病毒 相关 AGE 的当代数据有限。本研究旨在描述全因年龄和 诺如病毒 年龄成人急性住院期间的临床并发症特征。
设计与对象
使用 Optum 的去识别化 Clinformatics® Data Mart (Optum® CDM) 管理索赔数据库进行了一项描述性研究。使用 ICD-10-CM 诊断 代码识别 2022 年 7 月至 2024 年 6 月期间因全因 AGE 或 诺如病毒 特定事件住院的 18 岁以上成年人。
核心方法
使用经过验证的基于索赔的虚弱指数评估 60 岁以上成年人的基线虚弱程度。按年龄组(18-59 岁和 ≥ 60 岁)和是否存在基础医疗状况对首发 3 天内发生的急性住院期间的临床并发症进行评估。
主要结果
该研究包括 192,430 例住院的全因 AGE 发作和 2,589 例住院的 诺如病毒 编码发作。代谢紊乱、急性肾损伤、脱水和脓毒症是全因 AGE(分别为 57.2%、38.3%、29.2% 和 26.5%)或 诺如病毒 编码发作(分别为 62.0%、42.6%、41.9% 和 28.1%)患者中最常见的并发症。
解释与创新
作者解释说,患有全因和 诺如病毒 编码 AGE 的住院成人通常会出现肾脏、代谢和全身并发症。这些发现改善了严重 AGE(特别是 诺如病毒)的临床特征,并确定了不良结果风险最大的人群。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为有针对性的 监测 和严重 AGE 的风险评估提供信息,特别是在 诺如病毒 感染的成年人中,并强调需要改进高危人群的临床管理和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖行政索赔数据而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差并缺乏详细的临床信息。证据是中等的,因为它提供了对并发症的描述性见解,但没有建立因果关系。

Clinical complications during hospitalizations of all-cause and norovirus acute gastroenteritis among adults in the United States, 2022–2024

Authors: Christine Kim, John Shen, Brandon J. Patterson, Julia M. Baker, Caroline Bridges, Allison E. Olmsted, Rekha R. Rapaka, Ben Lopman, Daniel C. Payne, Evan J. Anderson
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Although norovirus is the leading cause of acute gastroenteritis (AGE) globally, contemporary data describing the clinical spectrum of hospitalized all-cause and norovirus-associated AGE are limited. We characterized clinical complications during acute hospitalizations among adults with all-cause and norovirus AGE. Methods We conducted a descriptive study using Optum’s de-identified Clinformatics ® Data Mart (Optum® CDM) administrative claims database. Adults aged ≥ 18 years with hospitalized all-cause AGE or norovirus-specified episodes between July 2022 and June 2024 were identified using ICD-10-CM diagnosis codes. Clinical complications during acute hospitalizations occurring within 3 days of the index episode were evaluated by age group (18–59 and ≥ 60 years) and presence of underlying medical conditions. Baseline frailty among adults aged ≥ 60 years was assessed using a validated claims-based frailty index. Results The study included 192,430 hospitalized all-cause AGE episodes and 2,589 hospitalized norovirus-coded episodes. Patients were predominantly older adults (median age, 74 years) with multiple underlying medical conditions. Metabolic disturbances, acute kidney injury, dehydration, and sepsis were the most common complications among patients with all-cause AGE (57.2%, 38.3%, 29.2%, and 26.5%, respectively) or norovirus-coded episodes (62.0%, 42.6%, 41.9%, and 28.1%, respectively). High frequencies of complications were also observed among younger adults with norovirus hospitalizations. Among adults aged ≥ 60 years, baseline frailty was substantially higher among patients with underlying medical conditions than among those without. Conclusions Hospitalized adults with all-cause and norovirus-coded AGE commonly experienced renal, metabolic, and systemic complications. Older adults demonstrated substantial baseline frailty, while severe complications also occurred among younger adults hospitalized with norovirus illness. These findings improve the clinical characterization of severe AGE, specifically norovirus, and identify populations at greatest risk for adverse outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Norovirus is the leading cause of acute gastroenteritis (AGE) globally, but contemporary data on clinical complications during hospitalizations for all-cause and norovirus-associated AGE are limited. This study aimed to characterize clinical complications during acute hospitalizations among adults with all-cause and norovirus AGE.
Design and population
A descriptive study was conducted using Optum’s de-identified Clinformatics ® Data Mart (Optum® CDM) administrative claims database. Adults aged ≥ 18 years with hospitalized all-cause AGE or norovirus-specified episodes between July 2022 and June 2024 were identified using ICD-10-CM diagnosis codes.
Core methods
Baseline frailty among adults aged ≥ 60 years was assessed using a validated claims-based frailty index. Clinical complications during acute hospitalizations occurring within 3 days of the index episode were evaluated by age group (18–59 and ≥ 60 years) and presence of underlying medical conditions.
Main results
The study included 192,430 hospitalized all-cause AGE episodes and 2,589 hospitalized norovirus-coded episodes. Metabolic disturbances, acute kidney injury, dehydration, and sepsis were the most common complications among patients with all-cause AGE (57.2%, 38.3%, 29.2%, and 26.5%, respectively) or norovirus-coded episodes (62.0%, 42.6%, 41.9%, and 28.1%, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that hospitalized adults with all-cause and norovirus-coded AGE commonly experienced renal, metabolic, and systemic complications. These findings improve the clinical characterization of severe AGE, specifically norovirus, and identify populations at greatest risk for adverse outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and risk assessment for severe AGE, particularly among adults with norovirus infection, and highlight the need for improved clinical management and prevention strategies in high-risk groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on administrative claims data, which may introduce bias and lack detailed clinical information. The evidence is moderate as it provides descriptive insights into complications but does not establish causal relationships.

诺如病毒 正在传播 – 如何识别症状以及该怎么做

Norovirus is spreading – how to recognise the symptoms and what to do
作者： Charlotte Lewis
摘要中文翻译诺如病毒 因其在人与人之间传播的方式而被称为“病毒世界的法拉利”,随着我们进入冬季,诺如病毒 再次出动。英国卫生安全局的报告强调,过去两周,英格兰的 诺如病毒(俗称“冬季呕吐病”)病例比前两周增加了 35% 以上。虽然这听起来令人担忧,但这仍然在本赛季的预期水平之内。总体病例数比一年中这个时候的典型病例数高出约 5%。苏格兰的病例有所下降,但威尔士的病例有所增加。诺如病毒 症状包括突发恶心、腹泻和呕吐,有时伴有发烧、胃痛、身体疼痛和头痛。大多数健康的成年人会在一到三天内康复。然而,免疫系统较弱的人、老年人和幼儿面临严重症状的风险较高。发生这种情况时,人们可能会生病更长时间,如果脱水,可能需要住院治疗。诺如病毒 具有高度传染性,一些研究表明少至 10 个病毒颗粒即可引起感染。当受感染的粪便或呕吐物中的微量病毒沾到食物、手或表面,然后转移到口腔时,病毒就会在人与人之间传播。人与人之间的密切接触也可以传播病毒。这可能有助于解释为什么病例在冬季达到高峰,因为此时人们在室内待在一起的时间更多。然而,您可以在一年中的任何时间捕捉到它。该病毒的抵抗力也特别强;它可以在不锈钢和陶瓷等坚硬的厨房表面上存活长达两周,并且需要 60°C 左右的温度才能杀死它。没有批准的 疫苗 或针对 诺如病毒 的特定 治疗。它迅速发展为电子...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 诺如病毒 的传播及其相关症状,强调需要认识和理解其季节性模式和公共卫生影响。背景强调了该病毒的快速传播及其被称为“病毒世界的法拉利”。
设计与对象
该研究分析了过去两周英格兰、苏格兰和威尔士报告的 诺如病毒 病例,并将其与典型的季节性水平进行比较。报告指出,与前两周相比,英格兰的病例数量增加了 35% 以上,而苏格兰则有所下降,威尔士则有所增加。
核心方法
该研究依赖于英国卫生安全局的数据,跟踪不同地区报告的 诺如病毒 病例。它还讨论了该病毒的 传播 机制,包括其通过受污染表面和密切接触传播的能力,以及其耐热能力。
主要结果
英国卫生安全局的报告显示,过去两周,英格兰的 诺如病毒 病例增加了 35% 以上,总病例数比典型季节性水平高出约 5%。苏格兰的病例有所下降,但威尔士的病例有所增加。
解释与创新
作者将病例的增加解释为与季节性预期一致,这表明英格兰的病例增加可能是由于冬季室内接触增加等因素造成的。他们指出,该病毒的恢复力和快速的 进化 给研究和控制工作带来了挑战。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 诺如病毒 监测 和预防的公共卫生策略提供了信息,特别是在密切接触常见的环境中。他们还强调,由于该病毒的快速 进化 和实验室生长困难,需要继续研究开发 治疗 和 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖于报告的病例数据而受到限制,这些数据可能无法捕获 诺如病毒 感染的所有实例。证据是温和的,因为它是基于观察数据而不是对照实验。

Norovirus is spreading – how to recognise the symptoms and what to do

Authors: Charlotte Lewis
Original abstract (English)
Described as the “Ferrari of the virus world” for the way it races from person to person, norovirus is once again on the move as we head into winter. Reports from the UK Health Security Agency have highlighted that cases of norovirus – commonly known as the “winter vomiting bug” – have increased by more than 35% in England over the past two weeks, compared with the previous fortnight. While that sounds alarming, this is still within expected levels for the season. Overall case numbers are about 5% above what’s typical for this time of year. Cases have fallen in Scotland, but Wales has seen a rise. Norovirus symptoms include sudden-onset nausea, diarrhoea and vomiting, sometimes accompanied by fever, stomach pain, body aches and headaches. Most healthy adults recover within one to three days. However, those with weakened immune systems, older adults and young children face a higher risk of severe symptoms. When this happens, people can be ill for much longer and may need hospital treatment if they become dehydrated. Norovirus is highly contagious, with some studies suggesting that as few as ten virus particles can cause an infection. It spreads from person to person when tiny amounts of virus from infected faeces or vomit get on to food, hands or surfaces and are then transferred to the mouth. Close contact between people can also spread the virus. This may help explain why cases peak in winter, when people spend more time together indoors. However, you can catch it at any time of the year. The virus is also particularly resilient; it can survive on hard kitchen surfaces such as stainless steel and ceramic for up to two weeks, and it takes temperatures of around 60°C to kill it. There are no approved vaccines or specific treatments for norovirus. It evolves quickly to e...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the spread of norovirus and its associated symptoms, highlighting the need for awareness and understanding of its seasonal patterns and public health impact. The background emphasized the virus's rapid transmission and its designation as the "Ferrari of the virus world.".
Design and population
The study analyzed reported cases of norovirus in England, Scotland, and Wales over the past two weeks, comparing them to typical seasonal levels. It noted that case numbers in England have increased by more than 35% compared to the previous fortnight, while Scotland has seen a decline and Wales an increase.
Core methods
The study relied on data from the UK Health Security Agency, tracking reported cases of norovirus across different regions. It also discussed the virus's transmission mechanisms, including its ability to spread through contaminated surfaces and close contact, and its resilience to heat.
Main results
Reports from the UK Health Security Agency indicate that norovirus cases in England have increased by more than 35% over the past two weeks, with overall case numbers about 5% above typical seasonal levels. Cases have fallen in Scotland but risen in Wales.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the rise in cases as consistent with seasonal expectations, suggesting that the increase in England may be due to factors like increased indoor contact during winter. They noted that the virus's resilience and rapid evolution pose challenges for research and control efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies for norovirus surveillance and prevention, particularly in settings where close contact is common. They also highlight the need for continued research into developing treatments and vaccines due to the virus's rapid evolution and lab-growth difficulties.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported case data, which may not capture all instances of norovirus infection. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational data rather than controlled experiments.

社区感染性肠道疾病的第三次研究:微生物学方法

The third study of infectious intestinal disease in the community: microbiological methods
作者： Christina Bronowski, Angela Hughes, Claire Jenkins, Gauri Godbole, Rachel M. Chalmers, Cristina Celma, Stuart Beard, Michael McDowell, Melanie Oates, Kristin Elwin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Infectious intestinal disease (IID) is a significant public health concern, with an estimated 17 million cases occurring annually in the UK. The third study of IID in the community (IID3 study) will provide updated estimates of the incidence and aetiology of IID in the UK.
设计与对象
该研究将从英国患有 IID 症状的受试者中获取粪便标本,这些受试者在 2023 年 5 月至 2025 年 9 月期间通过初级保健前瞻性地入组了两个 队列研究。
核心方法
利物浦临床实验室使用基于 PCR 的方法在两个独立的工作流程中对样本进行了 21 种病原体检测。第一个工作流程包括 EntericBio DX、Viral Panel 3 和艰难梭菌检测(Serosep,Crawley,英国);第二个是基于 3base ® 技术 [Genetic Signatures (GS),Newtown,澳大利亚] 检测产气荚膜梭菌 (cpa)、气单胞菌、卡耶坦环孢子菌和致泻大肠杆菌的一组检测。
主要结果
这些方法涵盖现代诊断和参考微生物学,代表了在英国人群中检测和表征引起 IID 的病原体的综合方法,并可作为未来研究的参考。
解释与创新
该研究将提供英国 发病率 和 IID 病因学的最新估计,为病原体的检测和表征提供全面的方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果将告知 IID 的 监测、诊断 和 治疗,为英国的公共卫生工作做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到初级保健前瞻性受试者招募的限制,这可能无法代表整个英国人口。证据强度中等。

The third study of infectious intestinal disease in the community: microbiological methods

Authors: Christina Bronowski, Angela Hughes, Claire Jenkins, Gauri Godbole, Rachel M. Chalmers, Cristina Celma, Stuart Beard, Michael McDowell, Melanie Oates, Kristin Elwin
Original abstract (English)
Infectious intestinal disease (IID) is a significant public health concern, with an estimated 17 million cases occurring annually in the UK. The third study of IID in the community (IID3 study) will provide updated estimates of the incidence and aetiology of IID in the UK. This protocol describes the laboratory methods employed by the IID3 study in primary diagnostic and reference laboratories. Stool specimens were obtained from subjects in the UK with symptoms of IID who were prospectively enrolled through primary care in two cohorts from May 2023 to September 2025. Specimens were tested for 21 pathogens using PCR-based methods in two separate workflows at Liverpool Clinical Laboratories. The first workflow comprised EntericBio DX, Viral Panel 3 and Clostridium difficile assays (Serosep, Crawley, UK); and the second a panel of assays for detection of Clostridium perfringens (cpa), Aeromonas spp ., Cyclospora cayetanensis and diarrhoeagenic Escherichia coli based on 3base ® Technology [Genetic Signatures (GS), Newtown, Australia]. Specimens that tested positive for Salmonella spp ., Campylobacter spp ., Yersinia spp ., Vibrio spp ., Shigella spp. and diarrhoeagenic E. coli were referred together with the corresponding bacterial isolate to the Gastrointestinal Bacteria Reference Unit, UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) for whole-genome sequencing and antimicrobial susceptibility testing. Samples that tested positive for Cryptosporidium spp., Giardia duodenalis and Cyclospora cayetanensis were referred for genotyping to the national Cryptosporidium Reference Unit, Public Health Wales. Norovirus-positive faecal specimens were referred for Sanger sequencing-based genotyping to the Enteric Virus Unit, UKHSA. These methods encompassing modern diagnostic and reference microbiology represent a comprehensive approach to the detection and characterization of pathogens causing IID in the UK population and serve as a reference for future studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infectious intestinal disease (IID) is a significant public health concern, with an estimated 17 million cases occurring annually in the UK. The third study of IID in the community (IID3 study) will provide updated estimates of the incidence and aetiology of IID in the UK.
Design and population
The study will obtain stool specimens from subjects in the UK with symptoms of IID who were prospectively enrolled through primary care in two cohorts from May 2023 to September 2025.
Core methods
Specimens were tested for 21 pathogens using PCR-based methods in two separate workflows at Liverpool Clinical Laboratories. The first workflow comprised EntericBio DX, Viral Panel 3 and Clostridium difficile assays (Serosep, Crawley, UK); and the second a panel of assays for detection of Clostridium perfringens (cpa), Aeromonas spp., Cyclospora cayetanensis and diarrhoeagenic Escherichia coli based on 3base ® Technology [Genetic Signatures (GS), Newtown, Australia].
Main results
The methods encompassing modern diagnostic and reference microbiology represent a comprehensive approach to the detection and characterization of pathogens causing IID in the UK population and serve as a reference for future studies.
Interpretation and novelty
The study will provide updated estimates of the incidence and aetiology of IID in the UK, offering a comprehensive approach to the detection and characterization of pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings will inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of IID, contributing to public health efforts in the UK.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the prospective enrollment of subjects through primary care, which may not be representative of the entire UK population. The evidence strength is moderate.

先天性巨结肠症:当肠功能障碍发生系统性转变时 - 病例报告

HIRSCHSPRUNG DISEASE: WHEN BOWEL DYSFUNCTION TAKES A SYSTEMIC TURN - A CASE REPORT
作者： Inês da Bernarda Rodrigues, Filipa Dias Costa, Afonso Sousa, Miguel Pereira, Carolina Sobral, Gabriela Pereira
摘要中文翻译背景:先天性巨结肠症(HD)是一种罕见的肠神经系统先天性疾病,由肠道发育过程中神经嵴细胞迁移受损引起。这会导致远端肠神经节缺失,最常影响直肠乙状结肠。其最可怕的并发症是先天性巨结肠相关小肠结肠炎 (HAEC),它是 死亡率 和发病率的主要原因。病例介绍:一名 4 岁男孩,有 HD 病史,两年前接受过 Swenson 拉通手术治疗,因一天腹痛、恶臭腹泻和呕吐而被送往儿科急诊科。没有记录到其他症状。体检未发现异常。给予口服止吐药和水化。在住院期间,他仍然昏昏欲睡,并伴有进行性腹胀,为此开始静脉输液以维持和补充持续的损失。最初的实验室测试和血培养没有异常。胃肠道分子检测 诺如病毒 呈阳性,粪便培养呈阴性。腹部X光检查显示肠袢和粪便明显扩张(图1)。六个小时内,他的病情迅速恶化,出现发烧、心动过速、外周灌注不良的症状和低血压。实验室重新评估显示严重代谢性酸中毒(pH 和 HCO3 低于可测量范围)、高乳酸血症(9.8 mmol/L)、急性肾损伤(肌酐 0.9 mg/dL;较基线增加 2.25 倍)和炎症标志物升高(C 反应蛋白 27.7 mg/dL 和降钙素原 >100 ng/mL),这些结果与表现为混合性严重 HAEC 一致。低血容量/感染性休克。复苏需要总共 60 mL/kg 的晶体推注,并开始使用头孢曲松和甲硝唑。通过放置鼻胃管进行胃肠减压,引流粪便物质,并进行直肠灌肠,将粪便爆炸性排出。他被转移到儿科重症监护室。进一步稳定需要额外的三次晶体推注和积极的液体治疗(高达 3500 mL/m2/天),并补充钙和钾,而不需要血管活性支持。抗生素调整为头孢噻肟、甲硝唑、庆大霉素。进行了连续直肠冲洗,排出了大量粪便。胃残留逐渐减少,腹胀明显改善。12 天后,患者出院,临床和分析结果稳定,肾功能正常。讨论:HAEC 是一种并发症,20-40% 的 HD 患者在手术前后都可能发生。及时的 诊断 和管理需要高度的临床怀疑。在初始阶段,临床表现是非特异性的,可以模仿 急性胃肠炎。然而,患者可能会迅速进展为混合性低血容量/分布性(脓毒性)休克。治疗依靠积极的液体治疗来稳定血流动力学和控制胃肠源头。广谱抗生素必须使用七到十天,覆盖厌氧菌和肠道革兰氏阴性菌。胃肠减压至关重要,包括放置鼻胃管、连续直肠冲洗和肠道休息。如果医疗管理不充分,或者出现肠穿孔、腹膜炎或积气,则可能需要手术。在本例中,HAEC(Pastor/Delphi HAEC 评分为 11)发生在手术两年后,可能是由 诺如病毒 感染引发的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
先天性巨结肠症(HD)是一种罕见的肠神经系统先天性疾病,由肠道发育过程中神经嵴细胞迁移受损引起。这会导致远端肠神经节缺失,最常影响直肠乙状结肠。
设计与对象
该研究是 病例报告 的研究,描述了一名有 HD 病史的 4 岁男孩,两年前接受了 Swenson 拔出手术治疗。他因一天腹痛、恶臭腹泻和呕吐病史到儿科急诊科就诊。没有记录到其他症状。
核心方法
给予口服止吐药和水化。在住院期间,他仍然昏昏欲睡,并伴有进行性腹胀,为此开始静脉输液以维持和补充持续的损失。最初的实验室测试和血培养没有异常。胃肠道分子检测 诺如病毒 呈阳性,粪便培养呈阴性。通过放置鼻胃管进行胃肠减压,引流粪便物质,并进行直肠灌肠,将粪便爆炸性排出。
主要结果
腹部X光检查显示肠袢明显扩张和粪便负荷。实验室重新评估显示严重代谢性酸中毒(pH 和 HCO3 低于可测量范围)、高乳酸血症(9.8 mmol/L)、急性肾损伤(肌酐 0.9 mg/dL;较基线增加 2.25 倍)和炎症标志物升高(C 反应蛋白 27.7 mg/dL 和降钙素原 >100 ng/mL),这些结果与表现为混合性严重 HAEC 一致。低血容量/感染性休克。胃残留逐渐减少,腹胀明显改善。12 天后,患者出院,临床和分析结果稳定,肾功能正常。
解释与创新
HAEC 是一种并发症,20-40% 的 HD 患者在手术前后都可能发生。在初始阶段,临床表现是非特异性的,可以模仿 急性胃肠炎。然而,患者可能会迅速进展为混合性低血容量/分布性(脓毒性)休克。治疗依靠积极的液体治疗来稳定血流动力学和控制胃肠源头。广谱抗生素必须使用七到十天,覆盖厌氧菌和肠道革兰氏阴性菌。胃肠减压至关重要,包括放置鼻胃管、连续直肠冲洗和肠道休息。
科研与公卫意义
及时的 诊断 和管理需要高度的临床怀疑。研究结果强调了认识到 HAEC 作为 HD 患者潜在并发症的重要性,即使在手术干预后也是如此,并强调需要采取迅速和积极的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。该证据基于单个 病例报告,其通用性有限。然而,研究结果与 HAEC 和 诺如病毒 感染的已知临床表现一致,支持适度的置信度评估。

HIRSCHSPRUNG DISEASE: WHEN BOWEL DYSFUNCTION TAKES A SYSTEMIC TURN - A CASE REPORT

Authors: Inês da Bernarda Rodrigues, Filipa Dias Costa, Afonso Sousa, Miguel Pereira, Carolina Sobral, Gabriela Pereira
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hirschsprung disease (HD) is a rare congenital disorder of the enteric nervous system, caused by impaired neural crest cell migration during intestinal development. This leads to aganglionosis of the distal bowel, most frequently affecting the rectosigmoid colon. Its most feared complication is the Hirschsprung-associated Enterocolitis (HAEC), the leading cause of mortality and morbidity. Case presentation: A 4-year-old boy, with a history of HD treated two years prior with a Swenson pull-through procedure, presented to the paediatric emergency department with a one-day history of abdominal pain, fetid diarrhoea, and vomiting. No other symptoms were recorded. Physical examination revealed no abnormalities. Oral antiemetics and hydration were administered. During hospitalization, he remained lethargic with progressive abdominal distension, for which intravenous fluids were initiated for maintenance and replacement of ongoing losses. Initial laboratory tests and blood cultures were unremarkable. Gastrointestinal molecular panel was positive for norovirus, with a negative stool culture. Abdominal radiography showed marked dilatation of bowel loops and faecal loading (Figure 1). Within six hours, he deteriorated rapidly with the onset of fever, tachycardia, signs of poor peripheral perfusion, and hypotension. Laboratory reassessment showed severe metabolic acidosis (pH and HCO3 below the measurable range), hyperlactatemia (9.8 mmol/L), acute kidney injury (creatinine 0.9 mg/dL; 2.25-fold increase from baseline), and elevated inflammatory markers (C-reactive protein 27.7 mg/dL and procalcitonin >100 ng/mL), findings consistent with severe HAEC presenting with mixed hypovolemic/septic shock. Resuscitation required a total of 60 mL/kg of crystalloid boluses, and ceftriaxone and metronidazole were started. Gastrointestinal decompression was performed via nasogastric tube placement, with drainage of faecal material, and rectal enema, with explosive discharge of stools. He was transferred to the paediatric intensive care unit. Further stabilization required three additional crystalloid boluses and aggressive fluid therapy (up to 3500 mL/m2/day), supplemented with calcium and potassium, without the need for vasoactive support. Antibiotics were adjusted to cefotaxime, metronidazole, and gentamicin. Serial rectal irrigations were performed, with abundant faecal output. Gastric residual progressively decreased, alongside marked improvement in abdominal distension. The patient was discharged after 12 days, clinically and analytically stable, with normalization of renal function. Discussion: HAEC is a complication which may happen both before and after surgery, in 20-40% of HD patients. A high index of clinical suspicion is required for timely diagnosis and management. During the initial stages, clinical presentation is non-specific and can mimic acute gastroenteritis. However, patients may rapidly progress to mixed hypovolemic/distributive (septic) shock. Management relies on aggressive fluid therapy for haemodynamic stabilization and gastrointestinal source control. Broad-spectrum antibiotics are mandatory for seven to ten days, with coverage of anaerobes and enteric gram-negative organisms. Gastrointestinal decompression is crucial, with nasogastric tube placement, serial rectal irrigations, and bowel rest. If medical management is not sufficient, or in case of bowel perforation, peritonitis or pneumatosis, surgery might be needed. In this case, HAEC (Pastor/Delphi HAEC score of 11) occurred two years after surgery, likely triggered by norovirus infection.

Question and background
Hirschsprung disease (HD) is a rare congenital disorder of the enteric nervous system, caused by impaired neural crest cell migration during intestinal development. This leads to aganglionosis of the distal bowel, most frequently affecting the rectosigmoid colon.
Design and population
The study is a case report describing a 4-year-old boy with a history of HD treated two years prior with a Swenson pull-through procedure. He presented to the paediatric emergency department with a one-day history of abdominal pain, fetid diarrhoea, and vomiting. No other symptoms were recorded.
Core methods

During hospitalization, he remained lethargic with progressive abdominal distension, for which intravenous fluids were initiated for maintenance and replacement of ongoing losses. Initial laboratory tests and blood cultures were unremarkable. Gastrointestinal molecular panel was positive for norovirus, with a negative stool culture. Gastrointestinal decompression was performed via nasogastric tube placement, with drainage of faecal material, and rectal enema, with explosive discharge of stools.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
HAEC is a complication which may happen both before and after surgery, in 20-40% of HD patients. During the initial stages, clinical presentation is non-specific and can mimic acute gastroenteritis. However, patients may rapidly progress to mixed hypovolemic/distributive (septic) shock. Management relies on aggressive fluid therapy for haemodynamic stabilization and gastrointestinal source control. Broad-spectrum antibiotics are mandatory for seven to ten days, with coverage of anaerobes and enteric gram-negative organisms. Gastrointestinal decompression is crucial, with nasogastric tube placement, serial rectal irrigations, and bowel rest.
Scientific and public-health significance
A high index of clinical suspicion is required for timely diagnosis and management. The findings highlight the importance of recognizing HAEC as a potential complication in HD patients, even after surgical intervention, and emphasize the need for prompt and aggressive treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The evidence is based on a single case report, which provides limited generalizability. However, the findings are consistent with known clinical manifestations of HAEC and norovirus infection, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

依库丽单抗治疗成年肾移植受者疑似晚发型非典型溶血性尿毒症综合征:病例报告

Eculizumab Therapy in Suspected Late-Onset Atypical Hemolytic Uremic Syndrome in an Adult Kidney Transplant Recipient: A Case Report
作者： Ji Yeon Min, Young Joo Jang, Hyosang Kim
摘要中文翻译背景和临床意义:非典型溶血性尿毒症综合征(aHUS)是一种罕见的、危及生命的、补体介导的血栓性微血管病(TMA),可能在肾移植后重新发生。由于其表现与同种异体移植功能障碍的更常见原因重叠,包括排斥、钙调神经磷酸酶抑制剂毒性和感染,诊断 可能具有挑战性。该疾病可能进展迅速,因此早期临床怀疑和及时的病因学评估至关重要。病例介绍:我们报告一名 52 岁男性,在 ABO 血型不合的活体供体肾移植后约 10 年后突然出现严重的移植物功能障碍。由于疑似慢性活动性抗体介导的排斥反应,在 治疗 治疗后,他的血清肌酐仍保持在约 2.0 mg/dL,但在门诊随访时增加至 6.57 mg/dL,在最近的 诺如病毒 感染后入院时增加至 8.47 mg/dL。初步评估考虑了移植物功能障碍的常见移植后原因,包括排斥、钙调神经磷酸酶抑制剂毒性和感染相关损伤。然而,随着肾功能障碍的进展以及并发贫血和血小板减少症变得明显,怀疑是TMA,并进行了有针对性的检查。乳酸脱氢酶升高、触珠蛋白降低、红细胞碎片和同种异体移植物活检结果支持 TMA。未检测到 HLA I 类或 II 类抗体,C4d 染色呈阴性,ADAMTS13 活性得以保留,产志贺毒素大肠杆菌检测呈阴性。基因检测发现了杂合 CFH 变异(c.3329G>C,p.Gly1110Ala),被归类为意义不确定的变异。根据临床、血液学和组织病理学结果,怀疑 aHUS 被认为是最有可能的 诊断。然而,不能完全排除 ABMR 相关的 TMA。过敏反应使血浆置换变得复杂,因此开始使用依库丽单抗。尽管肾功能没有恢复并且患者仍然依赖透析,但血液学异常随后得到解决。结论:该病例凸显了当移植物功能障碍迅速进展或仍无法解释时(甚至在肾移植后多年)考虑 TMA 的重要性。当怀疑补体介导的 aHUS 时,早期识别微血管病变特征并及时评估潜在病因尤其重要,因为尽管对 C5 抑制有血液学反应,但已确定的慢性同种异体移植物急性损伤可能会限制肾脏恢复。

Eculizumab Therapy in Suspected Late-Onset Atypical Hemolytic Uremic Syndrome in an Adult Kidney Transplant Recipient: A Case Report

Authors: Ji Yeon Min, Young Joo Jang, Hyosang Kim
Original abstract (English)
Background and Clinical Significance: Atypical hemolytic uremic syndrome (aHUS) is a rare, life-threatening, complement-mediated thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) that may occur de novo after kidney transplantation. Because its manifestations overlap with more common causes of allograft dysfunction, including rejection, calcineurin inhibitor toxicity, and infection, diagnosis can be challenging. The disease may progress rapidly, making early clinical suspicion and timely etiologic evaluation essential. Case Presentation: We report a 52-year-old man who developed abrupt, severe graft dysfunction approximately 10 years after ABO-incompatible living-donor kidney transplantation. His serum creatinine had remained approximately 2.0 mg/dL after treatment for suspected chronic active antibody-mediated rejection but increased to 6.57 mg/dL at outpatient follow-up and to 8.47 mg/dL on admission after a recent norovirus infection. Initial evaluation considered common post-transplant causes of graft dysfunction, including rejection, calcineurin inhibitor toxicity, and infection-related injury. However, as renal dysfunction progressed and concurrent anemia and thrombocytopenia became evident, TMA was suspected, and a targeted workup was undertaken. Elevated lactate dehydrogenase, decreased haptoglobin, fragmented red blood cells, and allograft biopsy findings supported TMA. No HLA class I or II antibodies were detected, C4d staining was negative, ADAMTS13 activity was preserved, and testing for Shiga toxin-producing Escherichia coli was negative. Genetic testing identified a heterozygous CFH variant (c.3329G>C, p.Gly1110Ala), classified as a variant of uncertain significance. Based on the clinical, hematologic, and histopathologic findings, suspected aHUS was considered the most likely diagnosis. However, ABMR-associated TMA could not be completely excluded. Plasma exchange was complicated by anaphylaxis, and eculizumab was initiated. Hematologic abnormalities subsequently resolved, although renal function did not recover and the patient remained dialysis-dependent. Conclusions: This case highlights the importance of considering TMA when graft dysfunction progresses rapidly or remains unexplained, even many years after kidney transplantation. Early recognition of microangiopathic features and prompt evaluation for the underlying etiology are particularly important when complement-mediated aHUS is suspected, because established acute-on-chronic allograft injury may limit renal recovery despite hematologic response to C5 inhibition.

Quality-by-design-guided development and qualification of an enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay for measuring human serum immunoglobulin G binding to norovirus genotype GII.4

保留,但未被证实安全:从冷冻历史到传染性证据及工艺验证的人类 诺如病毒 在冷冻食品中的存在

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

中国各地农业灌溉水中发现诺如病毒,冬峰进入高峰 - Bioengineer.org

Norovirus Found in Agricultural Irrigation Water Across China Shows Marked Winter Peak - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报2023 年 12 月至 2024 年 4 月期间在中国 27 个城市进行的一项研究发现,200 个采样灌溉水点中有 17% 存在 诺如病毒 污染,与春季相比,冬季的季节性污染明显增加。这项研究于 2026 年 11 月 21 日发表在 Bioengineer.org 上,使用了包括切向流过滤和逆转录定量实时 PCR 在内的分子 监测 技术来检测病毒 基因组。该研究强调了农产品污染的风险,特别是生菜、草莓和西红柿等经常生吃的农作物。家庭废水、畜牧业或受污染的地表径流产生的粪便污染将病毒颗粒引入灌溉网络,可能绕过收获后的清洗程序。该研究结果得到了统计评估的支持,并符合中国食品安全国家标准 GB 4789.42-2025,该标准要求病毒回收率超过 1%。水样中基因组 GII 的频率高于 GI,这与 2009 年以来中国临床胃肠炎 监测 中观察到的流行病学模式相符。这项研究强调了了解 诺如病毒 食源性 通过农业水道的路线对于有效预防 食源性 疾病的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2023年12月至2024年4月期间进行,发布日期为2026年11月21日。冬季采样期定义为从2024年2月4日开始。
地点与对象
该研究在中国27个城市进行,包括北京、桂林、常州、石家庄、赤峰、青岛、大理、柳州、信阳、新乡、福州、长沙、株洲、邯郸、济宁、兰州、珠海、长治和南宁。样品采集自河流、湖泊、地下水和市政自来水等各种农业水源。
事件
一项广泛的分子 监测 研究在中国 200 个灌溉用水点样本中的 17 个中检测到了 监测 污染,与春季相比,冬季的检测率出现了明显的峰值。该研究于2026年11月21日由Bioengineer.org报道,并根据分子监测的结果得到证实。
规模、影响与风险
17%的采样点发现了诺如病毒污染,且冬季季节性污染显着增加。该研究强调了农产品污染的潜在风险,特别是生菜、草莓和西红柿等经常生吃的农作物。这表明了解这些 传播 途径对于 食源性 疾病预防至关重要。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究采用了严格的方法,包括切向流过滤和逆转录定量实时PCR,并使用鼠诺如病毒菌株BJ10-2062作为过程控制。对结果进行统计评价,方法符合中国食品安全国家标准GB 4789.42-2025。研究结果得到了所提供证据的证实和支持。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Norovirus Found in Agricultural Irrigation Water Across China Shows Marked Winter Peak - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study conducted across twenty-seven cities in China between December 2023 and April 2024 found norovirus contamination in 17% of 200 sampled irrigation water sites, with a marked seasonal increase during winter compared to spring. The research, published by Bioengineer.org on November 21, 2026, used molecular surveillance techniques including tangential flow filtration and reverse transcription quantitative real-time PCR to detect viral genomes. The study highlights the risk of agricultural produce contamination, particularly for crops like lettuce, strawberries, and tomatoes, which are often consumed raw. Fecal contamination from domestic wastewater, livestock operations, or polluted surface runoff introduces viral particles into irrigation networks, potentially circumventing post-harvest washing procedures. The findings are supported by statistical evaluation and compliance with China National Food Safety Standard GB 4789.42-2025, which requires a viral recovery rate exceeding one percent. The elevated frequency of genogroup GII over GI in the water samples aligns with epidemiological patterns observed in clinical gastroenteritis surveillance in China since 2009. This study underscores the importance of understanding norovirus transmission routes through agricultural waterways for effective foodborne disease prevention.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Wednesday, November 25, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Norovirus Found in Agricultural Irrigation Water Across China Shows Marked Winter Peak by November 21, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 5 mins read 0 Norovirus Found in Agricultural Irrigation Water Across China Shows Marked Winter Peak Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram An extensive molecular surveillance study examining agricultural irrigation water across twenty-seven cities in China detected norovirus contamination in seventeen of two hundred sampled sites, demonstrating a marked seasonal increase in detection rates during winter compared to spring. The investigation, designed to assess the environmental presence of the virus at an essential interface of food production, employed tangential flow filtration and reverse transcription quantitative real-time PCR to detect viral genomes. Norovirus represents a major etiology of acute gastroenteritis across the globe, and agricultural water supplies have been identified as potential vectors capable of transferring enteric pathogens onto crop surfaces. By tracking both genogroup I and genogroup II lineages across a broad geographical distribution, the researchers aimed to clarify how irrigation sources serve as potential environmental reservoirs and evaluate the direct implications for agricultural produce safety. The structural characteristics and environmental resilience of norovirus contribute significantly to its capacity to persist within agricultural and aquatic systems. The viral capsid protein, designated viral protein 1, mediates host cell recognition and facilitates immune evasion while driving ongoing genetic diversification. Analysis of the gene encoding viral protein 1 categorizes noroviruses into ten distinct genogroups spanning from GI to GX. Within human populations in China, genogroups GI and GII represent the predominant infectious types. Strains belonging to genogroup GI infect humans exclusively, whereas genogroup GII displays a broader host range that encompasses animal reservoirs such as swine, where specific genotypes including GII.11, GII.18, and GII.19 have been identified, pointing to potential cross-species transmission pathways. Understanding transmission routes through agricultural waterways addresses critical aspects of foodborne disease prevention from an integrated One Health perspective. Norovirus exhibits high infectivity, with an infectious dose of fewer than twenty viral particles capable of inducing clinical gastrointestinal illness via the fecal-oral route. Global surveillance indicates that norovirus infections result in hundreds of millions of illnesses annually, accompanied by substantial mortality among vulnerable demographics including infants, elderly individuals, and immunocompromised patients. Because agricultural crops such as lettuce, strawberries, and tomatoes are frequently consumed fresh with minimal processing, contamination during the cultivation phase represents an acute vulnerability. Fecal contamination originating from domestic wastewater, livestock operations, or polluted surface runoff introduces viral particles into irrigation networks, where pathogens can adhere to plant surfaces or become internalized within vegetable tissues, potentially circumventing conventional post-harvest washing procedures. Discover more Soothe Digestion Check Symptoms Study Biology Source Farm Gear water To capture geographical diversity, the research team collected two hundred irrigation water samples between December 2023 and April 2024 across twenty-seven municipalities throughout China. The sampling coverage encompassed a broad variety of agricultural water sources, including rivers, lakes, groundwater systems, and municipal tap water utilized for irrigation purposes. The municipal distribution included one hundred seventeen samples from Beijing, ten from Guilin in Guangxi, seven from Changzhou in Jiangsu, seven from Shijiazhuang in Hebei, six from Chifeng in Nei Mongol, six from Qingdao in Shandong, six from Dali in Yunnan, five from Liuzhou in Guangxi, four from Xinyang in Henan, and three each from Xinxiang, Fuzhou, Changsha, and Zhuzhou. Additional paired samples were obtained from Handan, Jining, Lanzhou, Zhuhai, Changzhi, and Nanning, alongside individual samples from Chaoyang, Luoyang, Dalian, Wuhan, Jilin City, Changchun, Shenyang, and Suzhou. Temporal and spatial classifications were structured to evaluate environmental variation across distinct climatic divisions. Using February 4, 2024, as the chronological boundary, the investigators designated eighteen samples as winter collections and one hundred eighty-two samples as spring collections. Geographically, the sampling points were categorized relative to the Qinling Mountains-Huai River line, which demarcates northern and southern China. Under this division, forty-seven irrigation samples represented southern localities, whereas one hundred fifty-three samples were retrieved from northern regions. This structured division permitted statistical comparisons of viral prevalence across distinct regional hydrology and agricultural management contexts, allowing the authors to examine whether climatic shifts and geographical coordinates correlated with the frequency of detectable viral contamination. Discover more Study Marine Life Order Spring Water Water Methodological quality control was established using murine norovirus strain BJ10-2062 as an operational process control to monitor the efficiency and consistency of viral recovery. Spiking experiments complied with China National Food Safety Standard GB 4789.42-2025, which governs food microbiological examination and norovirus detection, requiring a viral recovery rate exceeding one percent for each evaluated sample batch. Serial ten-fold dilutions ranging from 10^8 down to 10 copies per microliter were quantified to construct a linear standard curve, which yielded a linear regression formula of Y equals minus 3.4705X plus 43.015 with a coefficient of determination of 0.9996, while demonstrating an analytical limit of detection of 100 copies per milliliter. The concentration protocol processed five-hundred-milliliter water volumes spiked with one milliliter of the process control suspension. Samples underwent preliminary vacuum filtration through sequential disc membranes with pore diameters of 0.45 micrometers and 0.22 micrometers to remove coarse particulates. Clarified filtrates were subsequently concentrated through tangential flow filtration utilizing a Viva Flow 50R system operated across a ninety-minute circulation cycle. Retained virions were eluted through a thirty-minute recirculation using thirty milliliters of a three-percent triethylene glycol monobutyl ether buffer containing beef extract powder, Tris, and glycine. Viral precipitation was achieved by adding a concentrated polyethylene glycol eight-thousand and sodium chloride solution, followed by overnight incubation and chilled centrifugation at ten thousand revolutions per minute for fifty minutes at four degrees Celsius. Ribonucleic acid was extracted from the resulting pellet using one milliliter of Trizol reagent prior to complementary DNA synthesis. Across the two hundred tested irrigation water specimens, seventeen samples tested positive for norovirus genomes, establishing an overall detection rate of 8.50 percent. When categorized by specific viral genogroups, genogroup GI was identified in eight samples, representing an individual positivity rate of 4.00 percent, whereas genogroup GII was confirmed in fourteen samples, yielding a positivity rate of 7.00 percent. A subset of five samples, accounting for 2.50 percent of the entire sample cohort, contained detectable genetic material from both GI and GII genogroups simultaneously. Cycle threshold values for confirmed positive reactions spanned from 35.05 to 39.25 for GI targets, and from 33.55 to 39.95 for GII targets. Statistical evaluation revealed a highly significant seasonal disparity in viral contamination between sampling periods. The detection rate of norovirus in agricultural irrigation water during the winter period was significantly elevated compared to the spring sampling period, with chi-square testing yielding a p-value of less than 0.001. In contrast, while the absolute positivity rate among southern samples exceeded that observed in northern samples, the geographic disparity did not achieve statistical significance, returning a p-value of 0.134. The elevated frequency of genogroup GII over genogroup GI observed in the water samples matches epidemiological patterns recorded across clinical gastroenteritis surveillance in China since 2009. Furthermore, the pronounced winter peak aligns with recorded human clinical outbreak dynamics, which historically cluster in China between November and January, suggesting that seasonal increases in community infections correspond with increased environmental viral loading in surrounding agricultural surface waters. Regional detection patterns exhibited local variations, including notable differences between municipal outbreak records and environmental water positivity. Among the one hundred seventeen irrigation samples collected from Beijing, only a single river water collection obtained from Huairou District tested positive for genogroup GII norovirus, suggesting that direct agricultural irrigation water might not serve as the dominant transmission driver in that municipality despite historically high clinical reporting. Conversely, river water samples collected from Shijiazhuang in Hebei Province demonstrated a high positivity rate of five positive samples out of seven tested, aligning with prior local pediatric diarrheal surveys reporting elevated positivity. These findings underline the necessity of sustained, integrated One Health surveillance across environmental, agricultural, and clinical domains to trace norovirus contamination dynamics and safeguard fresh produce supply chains. 诺如病毒 模型揭示英国儿童的高感染率和免疫印记 - Bioengineer.org

Norovirus Model Reveals High Infection Rates and Immune Imprinting in English Children - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报在英国进行的一项数学模型研究分析了 2007 年至 2012 年间 656 名儿童的 血清学 数据,揭示了 诺如病毒 GII.4 的高感染率和免疫印记效应。该研究估计总体感染率为每 1000 人年 205 例,每年变化范围为每 1000 人年 36 例至 486 例感染。五岁及以上儿童的感染率最高,达到每千人年 250 例,而一岁以下婴儿的感染率最低。该研究表明,建模的比率超过了之前基于疾病的 发病率 数据,这可能是由于广泛的无症状感染所致。这些发现支持免疫印记假说,即早期接触特定病毒变体会导致更强的抗体反应。该研究由 Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 23 日发表,数据是通过机会采样协议收集的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究发表于2026年11月23日。分析的数据跨度为2007年至2012年,其中2002年的攻击率达到峰值。
地点与对象
该研究主要针对英国儿童,特别检查了来自多个病毒季节的 656 名儿童的 血清学 数据。
事件
一项数学建模研究评估了英格兰儿童急性胃肠炎的诺如病毒 GII.4 血清学样本,揭示了高感染率和免疫印迹效应。该研究结果由Bioengineer.org于2026年11月23日报道。
规模、影响与风险
该模型估计 诺如病毒 GII.4 的感染率为每 1000 人年 205 例,年度变化范围为每 1000 人年 36 例至 486 例感染。五岁及以上儿童的感染率达到峰值,达到每 1000 人年 250 人。研究指出,模型感染率显着超过了之前基于疾病的 发病率 数据,表明存在广泛的无症状感染。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究使用专门的数学模型来模拟多变体抗体动力学并重建年发病率。研究结果基于 血清学 数据和模型估计,并为所有测量提供了可信区间。该研究提供了支持免疫印记假说的适度证据。

Norovirus Model Reveals High Infection Rates and Immune Imprinting in English Children - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A mathematical modeling study conducted in England analyzed serology data from 656 children between 2007 and 2012, revealing high norovirus GII.4 infection rates and immune imprinting effects. The research estimated an overall infection rate of 205 per 1000 person-years, with annual variations ranging from 36 to 486 infections per 1000 person-years. Infection rates were highest among children aged five years and older, reaching 250 per 1000 person-years, while being lowest in infants under one year. The study suggests that the modeled rates exceed previous disease-based incidence figures, likely due to extensive asymptomatic infections. The findings support the immune imprinting hypothesis, where early exposure to specific viral variants leads to stronger antibody responses. The research was published on November 23, 2026, by Bioengineer.org, and the data was collected through opportunistic sampling protocols.

Norovirus is a highly contagious pathogen in humans that causes diarrhoea and vomiting, spreading with particular frequency among young children. Winter incidence fluctuates annually, and earlier scientific investigations demonstrated that shifts in dominant viral variants typically precede elevated disease incidence across affected communities. To establish clearer biological mechanisms capable of refining epidemic forecasts, researchers examined unique norovirus GII.4 serology blockade measurements collected from 656 English children between 2007 and 2012. The underlying blood samples were gathered through opportunistic sampling protocols and evaluated using a specialized mathematical model simulating multi-variant antibody kinetics. This quantitative framework enabled investigators to reconstruct annual attack rates alongside age-stratified infection frequencies across multiple successive viral seasons. Discover more infectious disease Infection infections Model estimates showed wide annual variation throughout the evaluation window, yielding a range of posterior medians spanning from 36 to 486 infections per 1000 person-years. The annual attack rate peaked in 2002, coinciding directly with the epidemiological transition to the dominant Farmington Hills variant. Heightened attack rates appeared frequently alongside emerging norovirus strains, although researchers noted that this pattern did not occur under every single variant transition. Discover more Study Biology Soothe Digestion Check Symptoms infection Age-stratified analyses revealed that estimated transmission dynamics shifted markedly across child developmental stages. Infection rates were lowest among infants under one year of age, measuring 161 infections per 1000 person-years with a 95 percent credible interval of 121 to 210. Conversely, rates peaked among children aged five years and older, reaching 250 infections per 1000 person-years with a 95 percent credible interval of 213 to 289. Model parameters provided moderate evidence supporting the immune imprinting hypothesis, wherein individuals mount stronger antibody responses against the specific variants encountered earliest in life. The modeled infection rates significantly exceeded previous disease-based incidence figures from comparable environments, a discrepancy consistent with extensive asymptomatic infection. The authors concluded that combining multi-variant antibody data with kinetic models illuminates variant natural history to better guide epidemic preparedness. 研究绘制了全球网络 28 年儿科 诺如病毒 研究地图 - Bioengineer.org

Study Maps 28 Years of Pediatric Norovirus Research Across Global Networks - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报This study presents a bibliometric analysis of 2,161 publications on pediatric norovirus infections spanning 28 years, from 1998 to 2025. It reveals that global research on norovirus has evolved from early taxonomic identification to advanced genomic tracking, disease burden quantification, and vaccine design. Norovirus is a major cause of acute gastroenteritis and diarrheal outbreaks, responsible for over 700 million infections and 200,000 deaths annually, with children under five being the most affected group. The research is primarily transmitted through the fecal-oral route, including contaminated food and water. Despite its significant public health impact, no specific antiviral medications or vaccines are currently approved for clinical use. The study highlights the need for continued scientific inquiry and the development of targeted interventions. The research was conducted using data from the Clarivate Analytics Web of Science Core Collection, with publications identified between 1998 and 2025. The United States, China, and Japan were the top contributors, accounting for over 40% of global research output. The study also notes the structural evolution of international collaboration networks, with institutions like the University of Tokyo and Emory University playing key roles.
